# FURP story leads, 2026-11-10

Read-only queries against the FURP database (dev server, shared with furp.org). Every row carries a FURP page and the original record it was built from (IRS e-file object ID, SEC EDGAR accession, FEC transaction or committee ID, USAspending award, IRS revocation list). No figure is estimated; rows without a traceable source record were dropped and the count of dropped rows is stated per lead.


## Verdicts at a glance

| Lead | Title | Rows shown | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Texas nonprofit hospitals - top officer pay vs charity care and total revenue | 20 | partly answerable - top officer pay and total revenue are answerable for every TX hospital org from its latest Form 990; charity care is MISSING: Form 990 Schedule H is not loaded into the database and the e-file XML archive (/data/xml) is not on the dev server, so Schedule H Part I line 7a (financial assistance at cost) cannot be read; needs the Schedule H loader or the XML archive. 'System' is reported per filing organization (parent and subsidiaries listed separately); no parent/subsidiary roll-up exists in the data. |
| 2 | Government-funded nonprofits ranked by officer pay, and filings where paid people share a surname | 20 | List A: partly answerable - Form 990 Part VIII line 1e (government grants) is NOT loaded (no column in filings/expense_breakdown, XML archive not on dev), so the >50% test uses USAspending federal assistance obligations for the same fiscal year as a proxy; it misses state/local grants and can exceed 100% where award obligations and 990 revenue timing differ; needs line 1e from the e-file XML. List B: answerable from Part VII (surname = last token of the normalized name; name-only match, not a verified family relationship). Remaining noise to read past: UPMC group-return rows carry entity suffixes (PSV, MAG, FYO) as the last token, and a few filers list names surname-first (e.g. 'WILLIAMS DARREN'), so those groups are token matches, not shared surnames. |
| 3 | Revoked-status organizations that kept receiving grants | 20 | answerable for both sources - Schedule I grants (grantor-entered recipient EIN, exact match) and USAspending assistance awards to EINs on the IRS auto-revocation list, dated after the revocation with no listed reinstatement before the grant. Caveats: the IRS list's reinstatement column lags (several EINs are active in the current BMF, flagged per row), the first mass auto-revocation date 2010-05-15 covers many entities that never needed to file (government units, group-return subordinates, foreign affiliates), and Schedule I recipient EINs are as typed by the grantor (name mismatches flagged). |
| 4 | Top 100 universities by endowment proxy: president and highest-paid pay, latest year vs 5 years earlier | 100 | partly answerable (Schedule D Part V endowment is NOT loaded, so ranking uses Form 990 Part X line 16 total assets as the proxy; public universities that do not file Form 990 are absent; 6 of 100 presidents cannot be identified because the filing lists titles only as 'See Schedule O' or uses foreign titles like 'Vice-Chancellor'; 4 foreign universities have no filing 5 years earlier) |
| 5 | Schedule G fundraising events where direct expenses exceeded gross receipts, ranked by loss | 20 | answerable (figures are as filed on Schedule G Part II; the two largest losses, Midwest Challenge 2019 and Lions 2023, look like filer data-entry errors and should be verified against the original return before use) |
| 6 | 500 largest private foundations: payout rate and share of grants paid to DAF sponsors | 442 | answerable (payout rate = Part XII line 4 qualifying distributions / Part X line 5 net noncharitable-use assets, both from pf_payout for the same filing; DAF share = grant_links amounts whose recipient has organizations.daf_sponsor = true / all non-summary grants in the same filing; recipient matching in grant_links is name-based (match = FUZZY for all 990-PF links) and daf_sponsor flags any org reporting DAFs on Schedule D Part I, including community and university foundations, not only commercial DAF sponsors; 58 of 500 have no itemized grant rows in the latest filing and 10 have no pf_payout row) |
| 7 | Money chains between DAF sponsors and foundations (two hops, no operating charity) | 20 | answerable (for chains among DAF sponsors and private foundations as defined; caveats: hop amounts are multi-year sums from sim_grant_edges, hops are not time-ordered, and some 990-PF filers in the set are private operating foundations such as museums; the DAF-sponsor set was tightened to organizations whose DAF assets are at least 25% of total assets because organizations.daf_sponsor also flags universities such as Johns Hopkins that merely maintain DAFs) |
| 8 | Foundations tied to Warren Buffett's family | 4 | partly answerable: assets, payout and pay are complete for all four; grant recipients are complete for Susan Thompson Buffett, Sherwood and NoVo, but the Howard G. Buffett Foundation's e-filed Part XV grant lines carry no recipient names for 2011-2018 and FURP has no grant lines at all for its 2019-2025 returns (the lists are evidently attached as statements), so its recipients must be read from the original returns |
| 9 | Jen-Hsun & Lori Huang Foundation | 29 | answerable; caveat: Form 990-PF Part XV carries no recipient EINs, so the DAF-sponsor match is by recipient name (Schwab Charitable Fund, 'GE Force Fund' which the 2015 return itself labels a Schwab Charitable fund, Silicon Valley Community Foundation); the EIN-based check against organizations.daf_sponsor returns 0 rows for that reason |
| 10 | 'Fake fund' detector - fund-like names and SEC-registration claims with no Form D / ADV / IAPD / broker-dealer record | 118 | partly answerable (no size for state registrations; EINs mostly null; exact-key name match misses d/b/a and series names; ERA fund rows not loaded; no first-person SEC-registration claims found in 990 text) |
| 11 | Form D new private-fund filings by quarter, last 5 years (2021q4-2026q3) | 20 | answerable |
| 12 | Funds and nonprofits reporting exposure to Anthropic | 153 | partly answerable (no Schedule D Part VII investment line items loaded; names and narrative text only) |
| 13 | Top 50 donors to 2026-cycle federal super PACs and their nonprofit ties | 50 | partly answerable (nonprofit ties limited by FEC-to-people link coverage and name matching) |
| 14 | Defense-contractor executives and PACs giving to current Republican senators (cycles 2024 and 2026) | 16 | partly answerable (executives: yes, from FEC Schedule A; PACs: not answerable, needs FEC pas2 committee-to-candidate file) |
| 15 | US foundation grants to European NGOs and to US 'Friends of' charities of European institutions | 25 | partly answerable (Schedule F has region only, no recipient names; direct European NGO grants need country parsing) |


## Lead 1: Texas nonprofit hospitals - top officer pay vs charity care and total revenue

Set: organizations with state = TX and NTEE E20/E21/E22 (coalesce(nonprofit_ntee.ntee_code, organizations.ntee)) or a name containing "hospital", "health system" or "medical center"; latest non-superseded Form 990 per EIN; top pay = highest Schedule J Part II total paid by the filing organization itself (column "total_org"; related-organization pay shown separately so a system CEO is not counted again on every subsidiary return).

```sql
WITH hosp AS (
  SELECT o.ein, o.name, o.state, coalesce(nn.ntee_code, o.ntee) AS ntee
  FROM organizations o LEFT JOIN nonprofit_ntee nn USING (ein)
  WHERE o.state = 'TX'
    AND (coalesce(nn.ntee_code, o.ntee) IN ('E20','E21','E22')
         OR o.name ILIKE '%hospital%' OR o.name ILIKE '%health system%' OR o.name ILIKE '%medical center%')
),
latest AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (f.ein) f.ein, f.id AS filing_id, f.object_id, f.form_type, f.tax_year, f.total_revenue, f.xml_path
  FROM filings f JOIN hosp USING (ein)
  WHERE f.form_type = '990' AND f.object_id IS NOT NULL AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded, false)
  ORDER BY f.ein, f.tax_year DESC, f.filed_date DESC NULLS LAST
),
sj AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (filing_id) filing_id, name_raw, title, total_org, total_related,
         coalesce(total_org,0) AS pay
  FROM schedule_j WHERE filing_id IN (SELECT filing_id FROM latest)
  ORDER BY filing_id, coalesce(total_org,0) DESC
),
pp AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (filing_id) filing_id, name_raw, title,
         coalesce(comp_org,0)+coalesce(other_comp,0) AS pay
  FROM people WHERE filing_id IN (SELECT filing_id FROM latest) AND NOT coalesce(is_duplicate,false)
  ORDER BY filing_id, coalesce(comp_org,0)+coalesce(other_comp,0) DESC
)
SELECT coalesce(sj.pay, pp.pay) AS top_pay, coalesce(sj.name_raw, pp.name_raw) AS top_person, sj.total_related,
       coalesce(sj.title, pp.title) AS top_title, CASE WHEN sj.filing_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'schedule_j' ELSE 'part_vii' END AS pay_src,
       l.total_revenue, h.name, h.ein, h.state, h.ntee, l.tax_year, l.filing_id, l.object_id, l.xml_path
FROM latest l JOIN hosp h USING (ein) LEFT JOIN sj ON sj.filing_id = l.filing_id LEFT JOIN pp ON pp.filing_id = l.filing_id
WHERE coalesce(sj.pay, pp.pay) > 0
ORDER BY 1 DESC
```

Total rows behind the list: 186 TX hospital-set organizations with a Form 990 and a paid officer (latest filing per org); tax years covered: 2010-2025 (latest filing per org). Rows dropped for lacking a source object_id: 0 (the query requires object_id). Note: Texas Medical Center and Texas Hospital Association enter the set by name match and are not hospitals.

Verdict: partly answerable - top officer pay and total revenue are answerable for every TX hospital org from its latest Form 990; charity care is MISSING: Form 990 Schedule H is not loaded into the database and the e-file XML archive (/data/xml) is not on the dev server, so Schedule H Part I line 7a (financial assistance at cost) cannot be read; needs the Schedule H loader or the XML archive. 'System' is reported per filing organization (parent and subsidiaries listed separately); no parent/subsidiary roll-up exists in the data.


## Lead 2: Government-funded nonprofits ranked by officer pay, and filings where paid people share a surname

### List A - federal grants over 50% of revenue, ranked by highest officer pay (PROXY, see verdict)

Latest Form 990 (tax years 2022-2024, revenue >= $1M) per org; federal share = USAspending assistance obligations (usa_recipient_totals, kind = 'assistance') for the fiscal year equal to the 990 tax year, divided by Form 990 Part I line 12. Form 990 Part VIII line 1e is not in the database.

```sql
WITH latest AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (f.ein) f.ein, f.id AS filing_id, f.object_id, f.tax_year, f.total_revenue, f.org_name, f.state
  FROM filings f
  WHERE f.form_type = '990' AND f.tax_year BETWEEN 2022 AND 2024 AND f.object_id IS NOT NULL
    AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded, false) AND f.total_revenue >= 1000000
  ORDER BY f.ein, f.tax_year DESC, f.filed_date DESC NULLS LAST
),
fed AS (
  SELECT u.recipient_ein AS ein, u.fiscal_year, sum(u.total) AS fed_total, sum(u.n_awards) AS n_awards,
         max(u.top_agency) AS top_agency, max(u.top_award_url) AS top_award_url
  FROM usa_recipient_totals u WHERE u.kind = 'assistance' AND u.recipient_ein IS NOT NULL AND u.fiscal_year BETWEEN 2022 AND 2024
  GROUP BY 1, 2
),
sj AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (s.filing_id) s.filing_id, s.name_raw, s.title, coalesce(s.total_org,0)+coalesce(s.total_related,0) AS pay
  FROM schedule_j s JOIN latest l ON l.filing_id = s.filing_id
  ORDER BY s.filing_id, coalesce(s.total_org,0)+coalesce(s.total_related,0) DESC
)
SELECT sj.pay, fed.fed_total, l.total_revenue, round(100.0*fed.fed_total/l.total_revenue, 1) AS fed_pct,
       sj.name_raw, sj.title, l.org_name, l.ein, l.state, l.tax_year, l.filing_id, l.object_id,
       fed.n_awards, fed.top_agency, fed.top_award_url
FROM latest l JOIN fed ON fed.ein = l.ein AND fed.fiscal_year = l.tax_year JOIN sj USING (filing_id)
WHERE fed.fed_total >= 0.5 * l.total_revenue AND sj.pay > 0
ORDER BY sj.pay DESC
```

Total rows behind list A: 898 organizations meeting the >=50% proxy test; tax years 2022-2024. Dropped for missing object_id: 0 (required by the query).

### List B - two or more paid people on the same filing sharing a surname

People rows (Form 990 / 990-EZ / 990-PF Part VII) for tax years 2023+, compensation > 0, grouped by filing and by the last token of the normalized name; junk tokens (titles, suffixes, "see schedule", digits) excluded; at least two distinct first names required. Surnames are ranked by combined compensation; SMITH (643 filings), JOHNSON (436), KING, BROWN, JONES, WILLIAMS appear most often but do not dominate the 36,088 groups, so none were excluded.

```sql
WITH p AS (
  SELECT p.filing_id, p.ein, p.tax_year, np_last_name(p.name_norm) AS surname, split_part(p.name_norm, ' ', 1) AS first_tok, p.name_raw, p.title,
         coalesce(p.comp_org,0)+coalesce(p.comp_related,0)+coalesce(p.other_comp,0) AS comp
  FROM people p
  WHERE p.tax_year >= 2023 AND NOT coalesce(p.is_duplicate,false)
    AND coalesce(p.comp_org,0)+coalesce(p.comp_related,0)+coalesce(p.other_comp,0) > 0
    AND p.name_norm IS NOT NULL AND p.name_norm ~ '^[a-z]+( [a-z]+)+$'
    AND p.name_norm !~ '(^| )(inc|llc|llp|corp|company|associates|group|trust|bank|foundation|services|see|various|employees|schedule|former|current)( |$)'
),
g AS (
  SELECT filing_id, ein, tax_year, surname, count(DISTINCT name_raw) AS n_people, count(DISTINCT first_tok) AS n_first, sum(comp) AS comp_total,
         string_agg(name_raw || ' (' || coalesce(title,'') || ', $' || comp::bigint || ')', '; ' ORDER BY comp DESC) AS people
  FROM p WHERE surname ~ '^[a-z]{3,}$' AND surname NOT IN ('md','jr','sr','ii','iii','iv','phd','cpa','fache','esq','upm','upp','director','retired','year','request','former','current','officer','trustee','see','schedule','thru','through','dds','rn','cfo','ceo','group','return','various','employees','ann','marie','lynn','jean','lee','end','start','part','interim','acting','emeritus','chair','president','secretary','treasurer','cpa','mba','jd','do','dr','mr','mrs','ms','rev','dvm','dmd','pa','np','msn','mph','rph','cfa','cfp','pe','faia','aia','ret','term','left','joined','resigned','ex','exofficio','upc','pry','evp','svp','vp','employed','exec','executive','physician','staff','member','board','vice','senior','jr','sr','david','peter','kevin','john','james','michael','robert','mary','william','richard','thomas','mark','paul','steven','stephen','daniel','joseph','charles','christopher','matthew','andrew','brian','george','edward','scott','jeffrey','susan','linda','karen','nancy','patricia','elizabeth','jennifer','lisa','anthony','donald','kenneth','timothy','ronald','jason','gary','eric','ryan','jacob','larry','frank','jonathan','raymond','gregory','samuel','benjamin','patrick','alexander','jack','dennis','jerry','tyler','aaron','henry','douglas','adam','nathan','peter','zachary','kyle','walter','harold','carl','arthur','gerald','roger','keith','jeremy','lawrence','sean','albert','joe','christian','austin','willie','terry','jesse','ralph','billy','bruce','bryan','ann','anne','ana','amy','sarah','laura','julie','michelle','kimberly','deborah','jessica','shirley','cynthia','angela','melissa','brenda','emily','anna','rebecca','virginia','kathleen','pamela','martha','debra','amanda','stephanie','carolyn','christine','marie','janet','catherine','frances','ann','joyce','diane','alice','julia','judith','heather','teresa','doris','gloria','evelyn','jean','cheryl','mildred','katherine','joan','ashley','judy','rose','janice','kelly','nicole','judy','christina','kathy','theresa','beverly','denise','tammy','irene','jane','lori','rachel','marilyn','andrea','kathryn','louise','sara','anne','jacqueline','wanda','bonnie','julia','ruby','lois','tina','phyllis','norma','paula','diana','annie','lillian','emily','robin','peggy','crystal','gladys','rita','dawn','connie','florence','tracy','edna','tiffany','carmen','rosa','cindy','grace','wendy','victoria','edith','kim','sherry','sylvia','josephine','thelma','shannon','sheila','ethel','ellen','elaine','marjorie','carrie','charlotte','monica','esther','pauline','emma','juanita','anita','rhonda','hazel','amber','eva','debbie','april','leslie','clara','lucille','jamie','joanne','eleanor','valerie','danielle','megan','alicia','suzanne','michele','gail','bertha','darlene','veronica','jill','erin','geraldine','lauren','cathy','joann','lorraine','lynn','sally','regina','erica','beatrice','dolores','bernice','audrey','yvonne','annette','june','samantha','marion','dana','stacy','ana','renee','ida','vivian','roberta','holly','brittany','melanie','loretta','yolanda','jeanette','laurie','katie','kristen','vanessa','alma','sue','elsie','beth','jeanne')
  GROUP BY filing_id, ein, tax_year, surname
  HAVING count(DISTINCT name_raw) >= 2 AND count(DISTINCT first_tok) >= 2
)
SELECT g.comp_total, g.n_people, g.surname, g.people, f.org_name, g.ein, f.state, g.tax_year, g.filing_id, f.object_id, f.form_type, f.total_revenue
FROM g JOIN filings f ON f.id = g.filing_id
WHERE f.object_id IS NOT NULL AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded,false)
ORDER BY g.comp_total DESC
LIMIT 25
```

```sql
WITH p AS (
  SELECT p.filing_id, np_last_name(p.name_norm) AS surname, split_part(p.name_norm, ' ', 1) AS first_tok, p.name_raw, p.tax_year
  FROM people p
  WHERE p.tax_year >= 2023 AND NOT coalesce(p.is_duplicate,false)
    AND coalesce(p.comp_org,0)+coalesce(p.comp_related,0)+coalesce(p.other_comp,0) > 0
    AND p.name_norm IS NOT NULL AND p.name_norm ~ '^[a-z]+( [a-z]+)+$'
    AND p.name_norm !~ '(^| )(inc|llc|llp|corp|company|associates|group|trust|bank|foundation|services|see|various|employees|schedule|former|current)( |$)'
),
g AS (SELECT filing_id, surname, min(tax_year) ty FROM p WHERE surname ~ '^[a-z]{3,}$' AND surname NOT IN Total rows behind list B: 36,088 filing/surname groups on 32,606 filings; tax years 2023-2025. Dropped for missing object_id: 0 (required). Most frequent surnames among groups (before junk filtering): SMITH 643, JOHNSON 436, KING 365, BROWN 356, JONES 291, WILLIAMS 278, ANN 265, MILLER 241.
```

Verdict: List A: partly answerable - Form 990 Part VIII line 1e (government grants) is NOT loaded (no column in filings/expense_breakdown, XML archive not on dev), so the >50% test uses USAspending federal assistance obligations for the same fiscal year as a proxy; it misses state/local grants and can exceed 100% where award obligations and 990 revenue timing differ; needs line 1e from the e-file XML. List B: answerable from Part VII (surname = last token of the normalized name; name-only match, not a verified family relationship). Remaining noise to read past: UPMC group-return rows carry entity suffixes (PSV, MAG, FYO) as the last token, and a few filers list names surname-first (e.g. 'WILLIAMS DARREN'), so those groups are token matches, not shared surnames.


## Lead 3: Revoked-status organizations that kept receiving grants

### List A - Schedule I grants (from other nonprofits' Forms 990 / 990-PF) to EINs on the IRS auto-revocation list, grant tax year after the revocation year

```sql
SELECT gl.amount, gl.filed_name, r.legal_name, gl.recipient_ein, r.state, gl.tax_year, gl.purpose, gl.match,
       r.revocation_date, r.reinstatement_date, r.exemption_type,
       gl.grantor_ein, o.name AS grantor_name, gl.grantor_filing_id, f.object_id, f.form_type, gl.source, b.subsection, b.status
FROM grant_links gl
JOIN irs_revocations r ON r.ein = gl.recipient_ein
JOIN filings f ON f.id = gl.grantor_filing_id
LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein = gl.grantor_ein
LEFT JOIN irs_bmf_detail b ON b.ein = gl.recipient_ein
WHERE gl.match = 'EXACT' AND gl.amount > 0
  AND gl.tax_year > EXTRACT(year FROM r.revocation_date)
  AND (r.reinstatement_date IS NULL OR r.reinstatement_date > make_date(gl.tax_year::int, 12, 31))
  AND f.object_id IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY gl.amount DESC
LIMIT 20
```

```sql
SELECT count(*), sum(gl.amount), min(gl.tax_year), max(gl.tax_year), count(*) FILTER (WHERE f.object_id IS NULL)
FROM grant_links gl JOIN irs_revocations r ON r.ein = gl.recipient_ein JOIN filings f ON f.id = gl.grantor_filing_id
WHERE gl.match = 'EXACT' AND gl.amount > 0
  AND gl.tax_year > EXTRACT(year FROM r.revocation_date)
  AND (r.reinstatement_date IS NULL OR r.reinstatement_date > make_date(gl.tax_year::int, 12, 31))
```

Total rows behind list A: 30,982 grant lines totalling $3,051,558,749; grant tax years 2011-2025; dropped for missing grantor object_id: 0. (FUZZY name-only matches excluded.)

### List B - USAspending federal assistance awards (nonprofit-flagged recipients) first dated after the revocation

```sql
SELECT a.obligation, a.recipient_name, r.legal_name, a.recipient_ein, r.state, a.fiscal_year, a.first_date, a.last_date,
       a.agency, a.kind, a.award_type, a.program_title, a.permalink, a.award_key, r.revocation_date, r.reinstatement_date, r.exemption_type, b.subsection, b.status, a.recipient_uei
FROM usa_awards a JOIN irs_revocations r ON r.ein = a.recipient_ein
LEFT JOIN irs_bmf_detail b ON b.ein = a.recipient_ein
WHERE a.obligation > 0 AND a.first_date > r.revocation_date
  AND (r.reinstatement_date IS NULL OR r.reinstatement_date > a.first_date)
  AND a.kind = 'assistance' AND a.nonprofit
  AND a.permalink IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY a.obligation DESC
LIMIT 20
```

```sql
SELECT count(*), sum(a.obligation), min(a.fiscal_year), max(a.fiscal_year), count(*) FILTER (WHERE a.permalink IS NULL)
FROM usa_awards a JOIN irs_revocations r ON r.ein = a.recipient_ein
WHERE a.obligation > 0 AND a.first_date > r.revocation_date
  AND (r.reinstatement_date IS NULL OR r.reinstatement_date > a.first_date)
  AND a.kind = 'assistance' AND a.nonprofit
```

Total rows behind list B: 3,858 awards totalling $1,785,828,231; federal fiscal years 2010-2027; dropped for missing permalink: 0. State universities (e.g. UT Austin, Penn State) appear because their EINs are on the IRS list; they are government instrumentalities not required to file.

Verdict: answerable for both sources - Schedule I grants (grantor-entered recipient EIN, exact match) and USAspending assistance awards to EINs on the IRS auto-revocation list, dated after the revocation with no listed reinstatement before the grant. Caveats: the IRS list's reinstatement column lags (several EINs are active in the current BMF, flagged per row), the first mass auto-revocation date 2010-05-15 covers many entities that never needed to file (government units, group-return subordinates, foreign affiliates), and Schedule I recipient EINs are as typed by the grantor (name mismatches flagged).


## Lead 4: Top 100 universities by endowment proxy: president and highest-paid pay, latest year vs 5 years earlier

**Endowment is not loaded** (no Schedule D Part V table; `information_schema.columns ILIKE '%endow%'` returns nothing). Ranking uses the university's own Form 990 **total assets, end of year (Part X line 16 (B))** as the proxy. Universities = `college_orgs` relation `self`, match `EXACT`. Pay = Form 990 Schedule J Part II column (E) total (row (i), from the organization); 5-years-earlier = the Form 990 for tax year latest minus 5.

```sql
WITH uni AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT co.unitid, co.ein
  FROM college_orgs co
  WHERE co.relation='self' AND co.match='EXACT'
), latest AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (f.ein) f.ein, f.id AS filing_id, f.object_id, f.tax_year, f.total_assets_eoy, f.net_assets_eoy
  FROM filings f JOIN uni u ON u.ein=f.ein
  WHERE f.form_type='990' AND f.superseded IS NOT TRUE AND f.total_assets_eoy IS NOT NULL
  ORDER BY f.ein, f.tax_year DESC, f.filed_date DESC NULLS LAST
)
SELECT l.ein, o.name, o.state, l.filing_id, l.object_id, l.tax_year, l.total_assets_eoy, l.net_assets_eoy,
       (SELECT min(c.unitid) FROM uni c WHERE c.ein=l.ein) AS unitid
FROM latest l JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=l.ein
ORDER BY l.total_assets_eoy DESC
LIMIT 100
SELECT f.id, f.object_id, f.tax_year FROM filings f
WHERE f.ein=%s AND f.form_type='990' AND f.superseded IS NOT TRUE AND f.tax_year=%s
ORDER BY f.filed_date DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
SELECT sj.name_raw, sj.title, sj.total_org, sj.total_related
FROM schedule_j sj
WHERE sj.filing_id=%s
  AND sj.title ~* '(^|[^a-z])(president|chancellor|pres\.)([^a-z]|$)'
  AND sj.title !~* '(vice|v\.? ?p\.?|former|emerit|deputy|assist|assoc|provost|pro|advisor|office of|to the president)'
  AND NOT (sj.title ~* 'president of' AND sj.title !~* 'president of the (univ|college|instit)')
ORDER BY (sj.title ~* '^\s*(the\s+)?(president|chancellor)') DESC, sj.total_org DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
SELECT sj.name_raw, sj.title, sj.total_org, sj.total_related
FROM schedule_j sj WHERE sj.filing_id=%s
ORDER BY sj.total_org DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
SELECT p.name_raw, p.title, p.comp_org, p.other_comp FROM people p
WHERE p.filing_id=%s AND p.is_duplicate IS NOT TRUE
  AND p.title ~* '(^|[^a-z])(president|chancellor|pres\.)([^a-z]|$)'
  AND p.title !~* '(vice|v\.? ?p\.?|former|emerit|deputy|assist|assoc|provost|pro|advisor|office of|to the president)'
  AND NOT (p.title ~* 'president of' AND p.title !~* 'president of the (univ|college|instit)')
ORDER BY (p.title ~* '^\s*(the\s+)?(president|chancellor)') DESC, p.comp_org DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
SELECT p.name_raw, p.title, p.comp_org, p.other_comp FROM people p
WHERE p.filing_id=%s AND p.is_duplicate IS NOT TRUE ORDER BY p.comp_org DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
```

Notes: Endowment proxy = total assets end of year (Form 990 Part X line 16 (B)); it is not the endowment. President = highest-paid Schedule J row whose title contains president/chancellor/pres. and does not contain vice, former, emeritus, deputy, assistant, associate, provost, 'president of <sub-entity>'; fallback to Part VII when no Schedule J row. Highest paid = highest Schedule J Part II column (E) row (i) in the filing (fallback Part VII column (D)). Pay shown is from the filing organization only (column (i)); related-organization pay is in the JSON 'related' field only where available. Foreign universities (Toronto, Oxford, UBC, Quebec, Ottawa, Waterloo, Alberta, Tel Aviv) file Form 990 and appear in the top 100 by assets.

Verdict: partly answerable (Schedule D Part V endowment is NOT loaded, so ranking uses Form 990 Part X line 16 total assets as the proxy; public universities that do not file Form 990 are absent; 6 of 100 presidents cannot be identified because the filing lists titles only as 'See Schedule O' or uses foreign titles like 'Vice-Chancellor'; 4 foreign universities have no filing 5 years earlier)


## Lead 5: Schedule G fundraising events where direct expenses exceeded gross receipts, ranked by loss

Loss = gross receipts (Schedule G Part II line 1) minus direct expense summary (line 10), per event column; the Schedule G 'total' column (d) is excluded so events are not double counted. Superseded filings excluded.

```sql
SELECT (e.gross_receipts - e.direct_expenses) AS loss, e.gross_receipts, e.direct_expenses, e.net_income, e.name, e.slot,
       o.name AS org_name, e.ein, o.state, e.tax_year, e.filing_id, f.object_id, f.form_type
FROM fundraising_events e
JOIN filings f ON f.id=e.filing_id
JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=e.ein
WHERE e.gross_receipts IS NOT NULL AND e.direct_expenses IS NOT NULL
  AND e.direct_expenses > e.gross_receipts AND e.slot <> 'total'
  AND f.superseded IS NOT TRUE AND f.object_id IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY (e.gross_receipts - e.direct_expenses) ASC
LIMIT 20
SELECT count(*), min(e.tax_year), max(e.tax_year), count(*) FILTER (WHERE f.object_id IS NULL)
FROM fundraising_events e JOIN filings f ON f.id=e.filing_id
WHERE e.gross_receipts IS NOT NULL AND e.direct_expenses IS NOT NULL
  AND e.direct_expenses > e.gross_receipts AND e.slot <> 'total' AND f.superseded IS NOT TRUE
```

## Lead 6: 500 largest private foundations: payout rate and share of grants paid to DAF sponsors

Population: latest non-superseded Form 990-PF per EIN, top 500 by total assets end of year (Part II line 16 (b)). Payout rate = **qualifying distributions (Part XII line 4) / net value of noncharitable-use assets (Part X line 5)** from `pf_payout` for that same filing (the distributable-amount basis, Part XII line 4 / Part XI line 7, is in the JSON). DAF share = sum of `grant_links.amount` for that filing whose recipient EIN has `organizations.daf_sponsor = true`, divided by all itemized grants (`grants`, is_summary false) in the same filing. Part numbers are pre-2020 990-PF numbering; on 2020+ forms these are Parts IX, X, XI and XIV.

```sql
CREATE TEMP TABLE top500 AS
WITH cand AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ein FROM (
    SELECT ein FROM filings WHERE form_type='990PF' AND total_assets_eoy IS NOT NULL
    ORDER BY total_assets_eoy DESC LIMIT 5000) x
), latest AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (f.ein) f.ein, f.id AS filing_id, f.object_id, f.tax_year, f.total_assets_eoy
  FROM filings f JOIN cand ON cand.ein=f.ein
  WHERE f.form_type='990PF' AND f.superseded IS NOT TRUE AND f.total_assets_eoy IS NOT NULL AND f.object_id IS NOT NULL
  ORDER BY f.ein, f.tax_year DESC, f.filed_date DESC NULLS LAST
)
SELECT * FROM latest ORDER BY total_assets_eoy DESC LIMIT 500
SELECT t.ein, o.name, o.state, t.filing_id, t.object_id, t.tax_year, t.total_assets_eoy,
       p.noncharitable_assets, p.min_investment_return, p.distributable_amount, p.qualifying_distributions,
       g.total_grants, g.n_grants,
       d.daf_total, d.n_daf, d.daf_names
FROM top500 t
JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=t.ein
LEFT JOIN pf_payout p ON p.filing_id=t.filing_id
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
  SELECT sum(coalesce(gr.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(gr.noncash_amount,0)) AS total_grants, count(*) AS n_grants
  FROM grants gr WHERE gr.filing_id=t.filing_id AND gr.is_summary IS NOT TRUE
) g ON true
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
  SELECT sum(gl.amount) AS daf_total, count(*) AS n_daf,
         string_agg(DISTINCT r.name, '; ') AS daf_names
  FROM grant_links gl JOIN organizations r ON r.ein=gl.recipient_ein
  WHERE gl.grantor_ein=t.ein AND gl.tax_year=t.tax_year AND gl.grantor_filing_id=t.filing_id AND r.daf_sponsor IS TRUE
) d ON true
ORDER BY t.total_assets_eoy DESC
```

**500-foundation aggregate:** DAF-sponsor share of grants = **6.1%** ($3,377,518,344 of $55,276,746,135 itemized grants; 442 of 500 foundations have itemized grant rows, 333 of them made at least one grant to a DAF sponsor). Aggregate payout rate = **8.3%** ($66,681,255,903 qualifying distributions / $800,769,527,235 noncharitable-use assets, 483 foundations with both figures).

Total rows: 500 foundations (442 with a computable DAF share; full list in lead_06.json). Years: latest 990-PF filings tax years 2013-2025. Dropped for missing source record: 0 (query required object_id).

Caveat: Enterprise Holdings Foundation shows 100.9% because grant_links name matching attributed slightly more than the itemized grant total; treat shares near or above 100% as 'essentially all'. Gates Foundation's payout rate is not meaningful (its assets sit in the separate Gates Foundation Trust).

Verdict: answerable (payout rate = Part XII line 4 qualifying distributions / Part X line 5 net noncharitable-use assets, both from pf_payout for the same filing; DAF share = grant_links amounts whose recipient has organizations.daf_sponsor = true / all non-summary grants in the same filing; recipient matching in grant_links is name-based (match = FUZZY for all 990-PF links) and daf_sponsor flags any org reporting DAFs on Schedule D Part I, including community and university foundations, not only commercial DAF sponsors; 58 of 500 have no itemized grant rows in the latest filing and 10 have no pf_payout row)


## Lead 7: Money chains between DAF sponsors and foundations (two hops, no operating charity)

**Method.** Intermediary set = every EIN that filed a Form 990-PF (private foundations, 164,076 EINs) plus every DAF sponsor whose donor-advised-fund assets (Schedule D Part I line 4, latest non-superseded filing) are at least 25% of total assets (1,120 EINs). A first pass used the raw `organizations.daf_sponsor` flag (64,450 edges, $109,187,150,001, 2008-2025) but that flag includes operating charities that merely maintain DAFs (Johns Hopkins University: DAF assets $229,884 of $25.3B total), so the set was tightened. Edges come from `sim_grant_edges` (aggregated funder->recipient grants, all years). Chains are A->B->C with A, B, C distinct and every node in the set, ranked by the smaller of the two hop amounts. Each hop's source record is the largest single grant line for that pair in `grant_links` (joined to `filings.object_id`); the hop amount is the multi-year sum of all such lines.

**Headline.** Among intermediary-to-intermediary edges there are 20,399 pairs totaling $60,431,178,454 (2009-2025); 2,249 of those pairs are $1M or more, forming 69,935 two-hop chains (20,678 of which include a private foundation that is not itself a DAF sponsor). The top of the list is the big national DAF sponsors (Fidelity Charitable, Schwab Charitable (filed as "Donor Advised Charitable Giving"), National Philanthropic Trust, Silicon Valley Community Foundation, Goldman Sachs Philanthropy Fund) shuffling money among themselves: the largest chain is Silicon Valley Community Foundation -> Fidelity Charitable ($1,831,207,824, 2010-2024) -> National Philanthropic Trust ($1,905,192,552, 2016-2024). The largest chain that starts at a private foundation is Jasper Ridge Charitable Fund -> Schwab Charitable ($790,591,280, 2020-2022) -> Fidelity Charitable ($1,769,961,926, 2014-2024).

### SQL

```sql
-- 1. all intermediary->intermediary edges (count, sum, years)
WITH ds AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (d.ein) d.ein, d.daf_value_eoy, f.total_assets_eoy
  FROM daf_stats d JOIN filings f ON f.id=d.filing_id
  WHERE d.maintains_dafs AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded,false) ORDER BY d.ein, d.tax_year DESC, d.filing_id DESC),
s AS (SELECT ein FROM ds WHERE daf_value_eoy > 0 AND total_assets_eoy > 0 AND daf_value_eoy >= 0.25*total_assets_eoy
      UNION SELECT DISTINCT ein FROM filings WHERE form_type='990PF')
SELECT count(*), sum(amount), min(first_year), max(last_year) FROM sim_grant_edges g JOIN s a ON a.ein=g.funder JOIN s b ON b.ein=g.recipient WHERE g.funder<>g.recipient

-- 2. set sizes, edges >= $1M, chain counts
WITH ds AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (d.ein) d.ein, d.daf_value_eoy, f.total_assets_eoy, d.tax_year
  FROM daf_stats d JOIN filings f ON f.id=d.filing_id
  WHERE d.maintains_dafs AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded,false) ORDER BY d.ein, d.tax_year DESC, d.filing_id DESC),
s AS (
  SELECT ein, bool_or(kind='DAF') AS is_daf, bool_or(kind='PF') AS is_pf FROM (
    SELECT ein, 'DAF' AS kind FROM ds WHERE daf_value_eoy > 0 AND total_assets_eoy > 0 AND daf_value_eoy >= 0.25*total_assets_eoy
    UNION ALL
    SELECT DISTINCT ein, 'PF' FROM filings WHERE form_type='990PF') x GROUP BY ein),
e AS (
  SELECT g.funder, g.recipient, g.amount, g.n_grants, g.first_year, g.last_year
  FROM sim_grant_edges g JOIN s a ON a.ein=g.funder JOIN s b ON b.ein=g.recipient
  WHERE g.funder<>g.recipient AND g.amount >= 1000000)
SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM s WHERE is_daf), (SELECT count(*) FROM s WHERE is_pf), (SELECT count(*) FROM e), (SELECT count(*) FROM e e1 JOIN e e2 ON e2.funder=e1.recipient AND e2.recipient<>e1.funder), (SELECT count(*) FROM e e1 JOIN e e2 ON e2.funder=e1.recipient AND e2.recipient<>e1.funder JOIN s sa ON sa.ein=e1.funder JOIN s sb ON sb.ein=e1.recipient JOIN s sc ON sc.ein=e2.recipient WHERE (sa.is_pf AND NOT sa.is_daf) OR (sb.is_pf AND NOT sb.is_daf) OR (sc.is_pf AND NOT sc.is_daf))

-- 3. top 20 chains by smaller hop
WITH ds AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (d.ein) d.ein, d.daf_value_eoy, f.total_assets_eoy, d.tax_year
  FROM daf_stats d JOIN filings f ON f.id=d.filing_id
  WHERE d.maintains_dafs AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded,false) ORDER BY d.ein, d.tax_year DESC, d.filing_id DESC),
s AS (
  SELECT ein, bool_or(kind='DAF') AS is_daf, bool_or(kind='PF') AS is_pf FROM (
    SELECT ein, 'DAF' AS kind FROM ds WHERE daf_value_eoy > 0 AND total_assets_eoy > 0 AND daf_value_eoy >= 0.25*total_assets_eoy
    UNION ALL
    SELECT DISTINCT ein, 'PF' FROM filings WHERE form_type='990PF') x GROUP BY ein),
e AS (
  SELECT g.funder, g.recipient, g.amount, g.n_grants, g.first_year, g.last_year
  FROM sim_grant_edges g JOIN s a ON a.ein=g.funder JOIN s b ON b.ein=g.recipient
  WHERE g.funder<>g.recipient AND g.amount >= 1000000)

SELECT e1.funder AS a, e1.recipient AS b, e2.recipient AS c,
       e1.amount AS hop1_amount, e1.first_year AS hop1_first, e1.last_year AS hop1_last, e1.n_grants AS hop1_n,
       e2.amount AS hop2_amount, e2.first_year AS hop2_first, e2.last_year AS hop2_last, e2.n_grants AS hop2_n,
       least(e1.amount, e2.amount) AS min_hop, e1.amount + e2.amount AS total,
       oa.name AS a_name, oa.state AS a_state, sa.is_daf AS a_daf, sa.is_pf AS a_pf,
       ob.name AS b_name, ob.state AS b_state, sb.is_daf AS b_daf, sb.is_pf AS b_pf,
       oc.name AS c_name, oc.state AS c_state, sc.is_daf AS c_daf, sc.is_pf AS c_pf
FROM e e1 JOIN e e2 ON e2.funder=e1.recipient AND e2.recipient<>e1.funder
JOIN s sa ON sa.ein=e1.funder JOIN s sb ON sb.ein=e1.recipient JOIN s sc ON sc.ein=e2.recipient
LEFT JOIN organizations oa ON oa.ein=e1.funder LEFT JOIN organizations ob ON ob.ein=e1.recipient LEFT JOIN organizations oc ON oc.ein=e2.recipient
 ORDER BY min_hop DESC, total DESC LIMIT 20

-- 4. top 20 chains that include a private foundation (not a DAF sponsor)
WITH ds AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (d.ein) d.ein, d.daf_value_eoy, f.total_assets_eoy, d.tax_year
  FROM daf_stats d JOIN filings f ON f.id=d.filing_id
  WHERE d.maintains_dafs AND NOT coalesce(f.superseded,false) ORDER BY d.ein, d.tax_year DESC, d.filing_id DESC),
s AS (
  SELECT ein, bool_or(kind='DAF') AS is_daf, bool_or(kind='PF') AS is_pf FROM (
    SELECT ein, 'DAF' AS kind FROM ds WHERE daf_value_eoy > 0 AND total_assets_eoy > 0 AND daf_value_eoy >= 0.25*total_assets_eoy
    UNION ALL
    SELECT DISTINCT ein, 'PF' FROM filings WHERE form_type='990PF') x GROUP BY ein),
e AS (
  SELECT g.funder, g.recipient, g.amount, g.n_grants, g.first_year, g.last_year
  FROM sim_grant_edges g JOIN s a ON a.ein=g.funder JOIN s b ON b.ein=g.recipient
  WHERE g.funder<>g.recipient AND g.amount >= 1000000)

SELECT e1.funder AS a, e1.recipient AS b, e2.recipient AS c,
       e1.amount AS hop1_amount, e1.first_year AS hop1_first, e1.last_year AS hop1_last, e1.n_grants AS hop1_n,
       e2.amount AS hop2_amount, e2.first_year AS hop2_first, e2.last_year AS hop2_last, e2.n_grants AS hop2_n,
       least(e1.amount, e2.amount) AS min_hop, e1.amount + e2.amount AS total,
       oa.name AS a_name, oa.state AS a_state, sa.is_daf AS a_daf, sa.is_pf AS a_pf,
       ob.name AS b_name, ob.state AS b_state, sb.is_daf AS b_daf, sb.is_pf AS b_pf,
       oc.name AS c_name, oc.state AS c_state, sc.is_daf AS c_daf, sc.is_pf AS c_pf
FROM e e1 JOIN e e2 ON e2.funder=e1.recipient AND e2.recipient<>e1.funder
JOIN s sa ON sa.ein=e1.funder JOIN s sb ON sb.ein=e1.recipient JOIN s sc ON sc.ein=e2.recipient
LEFT JOIN organizations oa ON oa.ein=e1.funder LEFT JOIN organizations ob ON ob.ein=e1.recipient LEFT JOIN organizations oc ON oc.ein=e2.recipient
 WHERE (sa.is_pf AND NOT sa.is_daf) OR (sb.is_pf AND NOT sb.is_daf) OR (sc.is_pf AND NOT sc.is_daf) ORDER BY min_hop DESC, total DESC LIMIT 20

-- 5. per hop: largest single source grant line (run for each A->B and B->C)
SELECT gl.grant_id, gl.grantor_filing_id, f.object_id, gl.amount, gl.tax_year, gl.grantor_form, gl.source, gl.match, gl.purpose
FROM grant_links gl JOIN filings f ON f.id=gl.grantor_filing_id
WHERE gl.grantor_ein=%s AND gl.recipient_ein=%s ORDER BY gl.amount DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
```

Note: Lucas Museum of Narrative Art and Schmidt Ocean Institute file Form 990-PF (private operating foundations); they are in the set by the 990-PF rule but are arguably operating charities. Jasper Ridge Charitable Fund's grants were filed on Form 990 Schedule I (it has both 990 and 990-PF filings on record).

**Totals.** 20,399 intermediary-to-intermediary edges ($60,431,178,454), 2,249 edges >= $1M, 69,935 two-hop chains behind Table A and 20,678 behind Table B; data years 2009-2025. Rows dropped for missing source record: 0.

**Verdict:** answerable (for chains among DAF sponsors and private foundations as defined; caveats: hop amounts are multi-year sums from sim_grant_edges, hops are not time-ordered, and some 990-PF filers in the set are private operating foundations such as museums; the DAF-sponsor set was tightened to organizations whose DAF assets are at least 25% of total assets because organizations.daf_sponsor also flags universities such as Johns Hopkins that merely maintain DAFs)


## Lead 8: Foundations tied to Warren Buffett's family

EINs matched by `organizations.name_norm ILIKE '%buffett%' / '%sherwood foundation%' / '%novo foundation%'`: Susan Thompson Buffett Foundation 476032365 (NE), Sherwood Foundation 470824755 (NE), NoVo Foundation 470824753 (NY), Howard G. Buffett Foundation 470824756 (IL). Other matches (Rebecca Susan Buffett Foundation 364201771, Buffett Early Childhood Fund 201768874, Doris Buffett Legacy Foundation 813868962, Buffett Family Foundation 357337145) were not requested and are left out. All four file Form 990-PF, so "officer/trustee pay" is Part VIII line 1 (officers, directors, trustees) and line 2 (highest-paid employees); none has Schedule J rows.

**Headline.** Susan Thompson Buffett Foundation: $2,723,259,744 assets at end of TY2025, $1,589,388,023 qualifying distributions = 59.58% of noncharitable-use assets (the 5% minimum is $133,380,271); president Allen Greenberg paid $980,963. Sherwood: $977,795,342 assets, 41.78% payout; chair Susan A. Buffett paid $685,500. NoVo: $937,820,671 assets (TY2024), 35.24% payout; co-presidents Peter and Jennifer Buffett paid $1 salary each plus benefits; top recipient Tides Foundation ($510.1M over 2012-2024). Howard G. Buffett Foundation: $718,146,804 assets, 80.04% payout; Howard G. Buffett $0 salary; grant recipients not itemized in the e-file.

### SQL

```sql
-- filings
SELECT id, object_id, form_type, tax_year, tax_period_end, total_revenue, total_expenses, total_assets_eoy, net_assets_eoy, contributions, officer_comp_total, amended, superseded FROM filings WHERE ein=%s ORDER BY tax_year, id

-- payout
SELECT p.filing_id, p.tax_year, p.noncharitable_assets, p.min_investment_return, p.distributable_amount, p.qualifying_distributions, f.object_id, f.superseded FROM pf_payout p JOIN filings f ON f.id=p.filing_id WHERE p.ein=%s ORDER BY p.tax_year, p.filing_id

-- people
SELECT p.filing_id, p.tax_year, p.name_raw, p.title, p.avg_hours, p.comp_org, p.comp_related, p.other_comp, p.is_officer, p.is_director, p.is_trustee, p.source FROM people p WHERE p.ein=%s AND p.filing_id=%s AND NOT coalesce(p.is_duplicate,false) ORDER BY coalesce(p.comp_org,0)+coalesce(p.other_comp,0) DESC, p.name_raw

-- people_all
SELECT p.tax_year, p.filing_id, p.name_raw, p.title, p.comp_org, p.comp_related, p.other_comp FROM people p WHERE p.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(p.is_duplicate,false) AND coalesce(p.comp_org,0)+coalesce(p.other_comp,0) > 0 ORDER BY p.tax_year DESC, coalesce(p.comp_org,0)+coalesce(p.other_comp,0) DESC

-- schedj
SELECT filing_id, tax_year, name_raw, title, total_org, total_related FROM schedule_j WHERE ein=%s ORDER BY tax_year DESC, total_org DESC

-- clusters
SELECT DISTINCT pc.id, pc.display_name FROM people p JOIN person_cluster_members m ON m.person_id=p.id JOIN person_clusters pc ON pc.id=m.cluster_id WHERE p.ein=%s AND p.filing_id=%s

-- grants_latest
SELECT g.recipient_name, max(g.recipient_ein) AS recipient_ein, max(g.state) AS state, sum(coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0)) AS amount, count(*) AS n, max(g.source) AS source, max(g.purpose) AS purpose FROM grants g WHERE g.ein=%s AND g.filing_id=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false) GROUP BY g.recipient_name_norm, g.recipient_name ORDER BY amount DESC LIMIT 5

-- grants_total
SELECT count(*), sum(coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0)), count(DISTINCT g.recipient_name_norm) FROM grants g WHERE g.ein=%s AND g.filing_id=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false)

-- grants_all
SELECT g.recipient_name_norm, max(g.recipient_name) AS recipient_name, max(g.recipient_ein) AS recipient_ein, sum(coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0)) AS amount, count(*) AS n, min(g.tax_year), max(g.tax_year) FROM grants g WHERE g.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false) GROUP BY g.recipient_name_norm ORDER BY amount DESC LIMIT 5

-- grants_all_total
SELECT count(*), sum(coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0)), min(tax_year), max(tax_year), count(DISTINCT filing_id) FROM grants g WHERE g.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false)

```
(Each run per EIN; the "latest" filing is the newest non-superseded 990-PF. ProPublica link = https://projects.propublica.org/nonprofits/organizations/{ein}/{object_id}/full.)

Buffett Foundation's e-filed Part XV grant lines carry no recipient names for 2011-2018 and FURP has no grant lines at all for its 2019-2025 returns (the lists are evidently attached as statements), so its recipients must be read from the original returns


## Lead 9: Jen-Hsun & Lori Huang Foundation

EIN 261551239 ([FURP](https://furp.org/org/261551239)), Palo Alto, CA; matched by `name_norm ILIKE '%huang%' AND (name_norm ILIKE '%jen%' OR '%lori%')` (the only other match, Su-Ping Lee & Wei-Jen Huang Foundation 823741363, is unrelated). Officers: Lori L. Huang, President; Jen-Hsun Huang, Secretary & CFO; both $0 compensation every year (Form 990-PF Part VIII line 1). Schedule B lists Jen-Hsun and Lori Huang as the contributors.

**Headline.** Assets grew from $23,871,739 (TY2014) to $9,212,451,171 (TY2024) after $5,930,032,620 of revenue in 2024 (contributions $159,686,325; the rest is investment gain). Over 2014-2024 it reported 67 grant lines totaling $332,296,537 to 29 recipients; $253,144,862 (76.2%) went to donor-advised-fund sponsors, almost all Schwab Charitable ($252,867,862 across "Schwab Charitable Fund", "The GE Force Fund" and "GE Force Fund Schwab Charitable Fund"). TY2024 qualifying distributions were $126,336,116 against a distributable amount of $350,039,456 (1.76% of noncharitable-use assets).

### SQL

```sql
-- filings
SELECT id, object_id, form_type, tax_year, tax_period_end, total_revenue, total_expenses, total_assets_eoy, net_assets_eoy, contributions, amended, superseded FROM filings WHERE ein=%s ORDER BY tax_year, id

-- payout
SELECT p.filing_id, p.tax_year, p.noncharitable_assets, p.min_investment_return, p.distributable_amount, p.qualifying_distributions FROM pf_payout p WHERE p.ein=%s ORDER BY p.tax_year

-- by_recipient
SELECT g.recipient_name_norm, max(g.recipient_name) AS recipient_name, max(g.recipient_ein) AS recipient_ein, max(g.state) AS state,
   sum(coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0)) AS amount, count(*) AS n, min(g.tax_year), max(g.tax_year),
   array_agg(DISTINCT g.tax_year ORDER BY g.tax_year) AS years, array_agg(DISTINCT g.filing_id) AS filing_ids,
   bool_or(o.daf_sponsor) AS recip_daf, max(o.name) AS recip_org_name
   FROM grants g LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=g.recipient_ein
   WHERE g.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false) GROUP BY g.recipient_name_norm ORDER BY amount DESC

-- totals
SELECT count(*), sum(coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0)), count(DISTINCT recipient_name_norm), min(tax_year), max(tax_year), array_agg(DISTINCT source) FROM grants g WHERE g.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false)

-- daf_by_ein
SELECT g.filing_id, g.tax_year, g.recipient_name, g.recipient_ein, o.name, coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0) AS amount, g.purpose, f.object_id
   FROM grants g JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=g.recipient_ein AND o.daf_sponsor JOIN filings f ON f.id=g.filing_id
   WHERE g.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false) ORDER BY amount DESC

-- daf_by_name
SELECT g.filing_id, g.tax_year, g.recipient_name, g.recipient_ein, coalesce(g.cash_amount,0)+coalesce(g.noncash_amount,0) AS amount, g.purpose, f.object_id
   FROM grants g JOIN filings f ON f.id=g.filing_id
   WHERE g.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(g.is_summary,false) AND (g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%fidelity%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%schwab charitable%%'
     OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%silicon valley community foundation%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%vanguard charitable%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%national philanthropic trust%%'
     OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%donor advised%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%community foundation%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm ILIKE '%%charitable gift fund%%') ORDER BY amount DESC

-- people
SELECT p.filing_id, p.tax_year, p.name_raw, p.title, p.comp_org, p.other_comp FROM people p WHERE p.ein=%s AND NOT coalesce(p.is_duplicate,false) ORDER BY p.tax_year DESC, p.name_raw

-- schedb
SELECT d.tax_year, d.name, d.total_amount, d.filing_id FROM donors d WHERE d.ein=%s ORDER BY d.tax_year, d.total_amount DESC

```

**Totals.** 11 filings 2014-2024; 67 grant lines, $332,296,537, 2014-2024; 53 Schedule B contributor lines. Rows dropped for missing source record: 0.

**Verdict:** answerable; caveat: Form 990-PF Part XV carries no recipient EINs, so the DAF-sponsor match is by recipient name (Schwab Charitable Fund, 'GE Force Fund' which the 2015 return itself labels a Schwab Charitable fund, Silicon Valley Community Foundation); the EIN-based check against organizations.daf_sponsor returns 0 rows for that reason


## Lead 10: 'Fake fund' detector - fund-like names and SEC-registration claims with no Form D / ADV / IAPD / broker-dealer record

### Matching rule

Normalized name key = lower-case, every non-alphanumeric character replaced by a space, these tokens removed as whole words: the, llc, l l c, lp, l p, inc, incorporated, corp, corporation, co, ltd, limited, plc, lllp, llp, pllc, company, a, an, of, and; whitespace collapsed and trimmed. The word 'fund' is kept (so 'X Capital Fund I LP' and 'X Capital Fund II LP' stay distinct). A candidate is 'registered' if its key equals the key of any sec_formd.issuer_name (324,113 distinct keys), adv_filings legal or business name (63,316), iapd_firms legal or business name (49,241) or bd_firms.name (8,498), OR if its EIN equals a sec_formd.issuer_ein. Anything else is 'no match'. The rule is exact-key only: d/b/a names, series names and spelling variants (e.g. a nonprofit contractor listed as 'Strategic Investment Group' while the adviser is registered under a different legal name) show as no match, so every no-match row is a lead to verify on IAPD/EDGAR, not a finding of wrongdoing.

Fund-like name = contains the word 'fund'/'funds' AND one of: capital, partners, wealth, asset management, investment(s), holdings, capital/investment/wealth management, private equity, venture(s), hedge, opportunity/opportunities, growth; minus obvious charity/benefit-plan words (foundation, community, church, scholarship, memorial, endowment, charitable, relief, education, school, PTO/PTA, booster, fundrais*, employees, pension, retirement, benefit, welfare, union, club, association, society, league, loan fund, housing).

SEC-registration claim = Form 990 narrative text (research.filing_text per-field rows; the field='all' rows carry only a tsvector and no text) matching: registered investment advis(e/o)r | investment advis(e/o)r registered with/under | registered as an investment adviser | SEC registered / SEC-registered | registered with the (U.S.) Securities and Exchange Commission / the SEC | registered under the Investment Advisers Act. A second regex flags first-person claims ('we are / the organization is a registered investment adviser').

### SQL
```sql
SELECT src, count(*) FROM reg_names GROUP BY 1

SELECT t.ein, o.name, o.state, o.latest_total_assets, o.latest_total_revenue, t.tax_year, t.filing_id, f.object_id, t.field,
  substring(t.text from '(?i).{0,160}(registered investment advis[eo]r|sec[- ]registered|registered with the (u\.?s\.? )?(securities and exchange commission|sec)\M).{0,160}') AS snippet
FROM research.filing_text t JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=t.ein JOIN filings f ON f.id=t.filing_id
WHERE t.field='all' AND NOT t.superseded AND (t.tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','registered & investment & (adviser|advisor)') OR t.tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','sec & registered'))
  AND t.text ~* '(registered investment advis[eo]r|sec[- ]registered|registered with the (u\.?s\.? )?(securities and exchange commission|sec)\M)'
ORDER BY o.latest_total_assets DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3000

SELECT o.ein, o.name, o.state, o.latest_total_assets, o.latest_total_revenue, o.latest_filing_year, o.ntee,
  (SELECT string_agg(DISTINCT r.src, ',') FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k = trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(lower(o.name), '[^a-z0-9 ]', ' ', 'g'), '\m(the|llc|l l c|lp|l p|inc|incorporated|corp|corporation|co|ltd|limited|plc|lllp|llp|pllc|company|a|an|of|and)\M', ' ', 'g'), '\s+', ' ', 'g'))) AS reg_match,
  EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sec_formd d WHERE d.issuer_ein = o.ein) AS formd_ein_match,
  (SELECT f.object_id FROM filings f WHERE f.ein=o.ein ORDER BY f.tax_year DESC LIMIT 1) AS object_id,
  (SELECT f.id FROM filings f WHERE f.ein=o.ein ORDER BY f.tax_year DESC LIMIT 1) AS filing_id
FROM organizations o
WHERE o.name ~* '\mfunds?\M' AND o.name ~* '\m(capital|partners|wealth|asset management|investments?|holdings|capital management|investment management|wealth management|private equity|venture|ventures|hedge)\M' AND o.name !~* '\m(foundation|community|church|scholarship|memorial|endowment|charitable|relief|education|educational|school|university|college|hospital|library|museum|employees?|pension|retirement|benefit|welfare|union|club|association|society|league|trust fund|loan fund)\M'
ORDER BY o.latest_total_assets DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 300

SELECT count(*), count(*) FILTER (WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k = trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(lower(o.name), '[^a-z0-9 ]', ' ', 'g'), '\m(the|llc|l l c|lp|l p|inc|incorporated|corp|corporation|co|ltd|limited|plc|lllp|llp|pllc|company|a|an|of|and)\M', ' ', 'g'), '\s+', ' ', 'g'))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sec_formd d WHERE d.issuer_ein=o.ein)) FROM organizations o WHERE o.name ~* '\mfunds?\M' AND o.name ~* '\m(capital|partners|wealth|asset management|investments?|holdings|capital management|investment management|wealth management|private equity|venture|ventures|hedge)\M' AND o.name !~* '\m(foundation|community|church|scholarship|memorial|endowment|charitable|relief|education|educational|school|university|college|hospital|library|museum|employees?|pension|retirement|benefit|welfare|union|club|association|society|league|trust fund|loan fund)\M'

SELECT count(*), count(*) FILTER (WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k=b.k)), count(*) FILTER (WHERE b.ein IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sec_formd d WHERE d.issuer_ein=b.ein)), count(*) FILTER (WHERE b.status ILIKE 'active%'), count(*) FILTER (WHERE b.status ILIKE 'active%' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k=b.k)) FROM biz_fund b

SELECT state, count(*), count(*) FILTER (WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k=b.k)) FROM biz_fund b GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC

SELECT status, count(*) FROM biz_fund GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 12

SELECT entity_type, count(*) FROM biz_fund GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 12

SELECT b.state, b.entity_id, b.name, b.entity_type, b.status, b.formed, b.agent_name, b.jurisdiction, b.source, b.ein
FROM biz_fund b WHERE b.status ILIKE 'active%' AND b.formed BETWEEN DATE '2015-01-01' AND DATE '2024-12-31'
  AND b.name ~* '\m(l\.? ?p\.?|limited partnership)$' AND b.name ~* '\m(capital|partners|ventures?|private equity|hedge|opportunit(y|ies)|growth)\M' AND b.name ~* '\m([ivx]+|[0-9]+|[a-c])\M'
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k=b.k)
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sec_formd d WHERE d.issuer_ein IS NOT NULL AND d.issuer_ein=b.ein)
ORDER BY b.formed DESC LIMIT 40

SELECT count(*) FROM biz_fund b WHERE b.status ILIKE 'active%' AND b.formed BETWEEN DATE '2015-01-01' AND DATE '2024-12-31'
  AND b.name ~* '\m(l\.? ?p\.?|limited partnership)$' AND b.name ~* '\m(capital|partners|ventures?|private equity|hedge|opportunit(y|ies)|growth)\M' AND b.name ~* '\m([ivx]+|[0-9]+|[a-c])\M'
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k=b.k) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sec_formd d WHERE d.issuer_ein IS NOT NULL AND d.issuer_ein=b.ein)

SELECT c.name_raw, sum(c.compensation) AS paid, count(DISTINCT c.ein) AS n_orgs, max(c.tax_year), min(c.tax_year), max(c.filing_id), max(c.ein),
  (SELECT string_agg(DISTINCT r.src, ',') FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k = trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(lower(c.name_raw), '[^a-z0-9 ]', ' ', 'g'), '\m(the|llc|l l c|lp|l p|inc|incorporated|corp|corporation|co|ltd|limited|plc|lllp|llp|pllc|company|a|an|of|and)\M', ' ', 'g'), '\s+', ' ', 'g'))) AS reg_match
FROM contractors c WHERE c.service_description ~* '\m(investment|asset|portfolio|fund) (management|manager|advis\w+|consult\w+)\M'
  AND c.name_raw ~* '\m(capital|partners|wealth|asset management|investments?|holdings|advisors?|advisers?)\M'
GROUP BY c.name_raw ORDER BY paid DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 400

SELECT DISTINCT ON (t.ein) t.ein, o.name, o.state, o.latest_total_assets, o.latest_total_revenue, t.tax_year, t.filing_id, f.object_id, t.form_type, t.field,
  substring(t.text from ('(?i)(.{0,200}' || %s || '.{0,200})')) AS snippet,
  (SELECT string_agg(DISTINCT r.src, ',') FROM reg_names r WHERE r.k = trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(lower(o.name), '[^a-z0-9 ]', ' ', 'g'), '\m(the|llc|l l c|lp|l p|inc|incorporated|corp|corporation|co|ltd|limited|plc|lllp|llp|pllc|company|a|an|of|and)\M', ' ', 'g'), '\s+', ' ', 'g'))) AS reg_match,
  EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sec_formd d WHERE d.issuer_ein = o.ein) AS formd_ein,
  t.text ~* '\m(we|it|the (organization|company|corporation|foundation|fund|institute|trust)|[a-z]+) (is|are|became|remains) (an? |now an? )?(sec[- ]|federally[- ])?registered investment advis[eo]r' OR t.text ~* '\m(organization|company|corporation|fund|foundation|we|it) (is|are) registered (with|under) the (u\.?s\.? )?(securities (and|&) exchange commission|sec|investment advisers act)' AS self_claim
FROM research.filing_text t JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=t.ein JOIN filings f ON f.id=t.filing_id
WHERE t.field <> 'all' AND NOT coalesce(t.superseded,false) AND (t.tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','registered & investment & (adviser|advisor)') OR t.tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','sec & registered'))
  AND t.text ~* %s
ORDER BY t.ein, t.tax_year DESC

SELECT state, entity_id, name, entity_type, status, formed, agent_name, jurisdiction, source, ein, city, addr_state FROM biz_entities WHERE name ILIKE %s LIMIT 50

SELECT ein, name, state, latest_filing_year, latest_total_revenue, latest_total_assets FROM organizations WHERE name ILIKE %s LIMIT 50

SELECT accession, cik, issuer_name, filing_date, fund_type, offering_amount, state FROM sec_formd WHERE issuer_name ILIKE %s LIMIT 50
```

### Counts

- State business registrations with a fund-like name (biz_entities, 30 states): **14,846**; name-matched to a Form D / ADV / IAPD / BD record: 2,642; EIN-matched to a Form D issuer: 0 (biz_entities EINs are mostly null); currently active: 8,688; **active with no match: 6,581**.
- Strongest-claim subset (active limited partnership, name ends in LP / Limited Partnership, numbered or lettered fund name, formed 2015-2024, no match by name or EIN): **291** (40 most recent listed below).
- Nonprofits (organizations) with a fund-like name: **170**; matched to a registration: 4; no match: 166.
- Nonprofits whose 990 narrative text contains an SEC-registration / registered-investment-adviser phrase: **38** organizations (one row each, latest year); 3 of them match a registration by name/EIN; 6 trip the first-person regex. On reading, every snippet describes a third-party adviser, a contractor, or an industry topic, not the filer itself (see table C).
- Nonprofit contractors described as investment/asset/portfolio/fund managers or advisers with a fund-like or adviser-like name: 400 distinct names in the top 400 by compensation; 109 have no exact-key match.

By state (fund-like registrations / name-matched): FL 3,556/383; NY 3,162/799; TX 3,154/876; CO 1,446/140; CT 877/42; PA 759/88; MN 696/93; VA 449/49; DC 347/59; OR 196/67; IA 78/19; MA 49/9; CA 17/4; AK 14/1.

Summary for the two names: BG Wealth Sharing LTD does have an SEC record, but only as an Exempt Reporting Adviser (Form ADV filed 2025-08-15, one private fund, $78.96M gross assets reported) and it is absent from the IAPD firm feed and delinquent in Colorado; no Form D or broker-dealer record exists under any 'BG Wealth' name. DSJ Exchange PTY Ltd has exactly one SEC record: a Rule 506(c) Form D for a $100M 'Investing' offering with $0 sold and 0 investors, one director, and a Colorado registration that is delinquent; no ADV, IAPD or broker-dealer record.

Total rows: 118 rows listed in the JSON (A 20, B 40, C 38, D 20); populations behind them: 14,846 fund-like registrations (of 34.9M biz_entities rows), 170 fund-like nonprofits (of 3.38M organizations), 38 990-text claim hits (of 7.5M filing_text documents). Years: registrations through 2026-05; 990 text tax years 2010-2025; Form D 2008q1-2026q3; Form ADV 2000-2026.

Verdict: partly answerable. The name-based detector and the per-table check of BG Wealth / DSJ Exchange are answerable as above. Missing: (1) registrations carry no size, so the state-registry list cannot be ranked by assets; (2) biz_entities EINs are mostly null, so EIN matching to Form D rarely applies; (3) the exact-key name rule misses d/b/a and series names (a fuzzy match or a manual IAPD lookup is needed before calling any row unregistered); (4) ERA private-fund rows (Form ADV Schedule D 7.B.(1)) are not loaded, so BG Wealth Sharing's fund name is unavailable; (5) no first-person 990 claims of SEC registration were found, so that part of the detector yields third-party references only. Dropped: 0 rows (every listed row has a registry, EDGAR, IAPD or IRS e-file identifier).


## Lead 11: Form D new private-fund filings by quarter, last 5 years (2021q4-2026q3)

Scope: `sec_formd` rows with `is_amendment = false` (new filings only, no amendments) and `fund_type` in the four values present in the data: Venture Capital Fund, Private Equity Fund, Hedge Fund, Other Investment Fund; `filing_date` 2021-10-01 to 2026-09-30 (20 quarters). Rows where the issuer ticked "Indefinite" for total offering amount (`offering_indefinite`) are counted separately; their `amount_sold` is shown instead of an offering amount. Amounts are as filed (Form D Item 13), not verified or converted; several of the largest look like filer unit errors (see note below).

### SQL
```sql
SELECT quarter,
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE NOT coalesce(offering_indefinite,false)) AS n_fixed,
  sum(offering_amount) FILTER (WHERE NOT coalesce(offering_indefinite,false)) AS offering_fixed,
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE coalesce(offering_indefinite,false)) AS n_indef,
  sum(amount_sold) FILTER (WHERE coalesce(offering_indefinite,false)) AS sold_indef,
  sum(amount_sold) AS sold_all,
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE fund_type='Venture Capital Fund') AS n_vc,
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE fund_type='Private Equity Fund') AS n_pe,
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE fund_type='Hedge Fund') AS n_hedge,
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE fund_type='Other Investment Fund') AS n_other
FROM sec_formd
WHERE is_amendment = false AND fund_type IN ('Venture Capital Fund','Private Equity Fund','Hedge Fund','Other Investment Fund')
  AND filing_date >= DATE '2021-10-01' AND filing_date <= DATE '2026-09-30'
GROUP BY quarter ORDER BY quarter

SELECT accession, cik, issuer_name, fund_type, filing_date, offering_amount, amount_sold, investors, state, industry
FROM sec_formd
WHERE is_amendment = false AND fund_type IN ('Venture Capital Fund','Private Equity Fund','Hedge Fund','Other Investment Fund')
  AND NOT coalesce(offering_indefinite,false) AND filing_date >= DATE '2021-10-01' AND filing_date <= DATE '2026-09-30'
ORDER BY offering_amount DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 20

WITH base AS (
  SELECT extract(year FROM filing_date)::int AS yr, accession, offering_amount
  FROM sec_formd
  WHERE is_amendment = false AND fund_type IN ('Venture Capital Fund','Private Equity Fund','Hedge Fund','Other Investment Fund')
    AND NOT coalesce(offering_indefinite,false) AND offering_amount IS NOT NULL
    AND filing_date >= DATE '2021-10-01' AND filing_date <= DATE '2026-09-30'),
ranked AS (SELECT yr, accession, offering_amount, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY yr ORDER BY offering_amount DESC) AS rn FROM base)
SELECT yr, count(*) AS n, sum(offering_amount) AS total,
       sum(offering_amount) FILTER (WHERE rn <= 20) AS top20,
       round(100.0 * sum(offering_amount) FILTER (WHERE rn <= 20) / nullif(sum(offering_amount),0), 2) AS top20_pct
FROM ranked GROUP BY yr ORDER BY yr

SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM sec_formd WHERE is_amendment=false AND fund_type IN ('Venture Capital Fund','Private Equity Fund','Hedge Fund','Other Investment Fund') AND filing_date BETWEEN DATE '2021-10-01' AND DATE '2026-09-30'),
 (SELECT count(*) FROM sec_formd WHERE is_amendment=false AND fund_type IN ('Venture Capital Fund','Private Equity Fund','Hedge Fund','Other Investment Fund') AND filing_date BETWEEN DATE '2021-10-01' AND DATE '2026-09-30' AND NOT coalesce(offering_indefinite,false) AND offering_amount IS NULL)
```

Excluding nothing and estimating nothing, the reader should treat them as probable unit errors by the filer and verify on EDGAR. State N4 = Luxembourg, U0/K3 = foreign codes in the SEC state table.

Total rows behind the aggregates: 92,529 new (non-amendment) private-fund Form D filings, 2021-10-01 to 2026-09-30 (51,439 with a fixed offering amount, 41,090 indefinite); 0 fixed-amount rows had a null amount. Years covered: 2021q4-2026q3 (sec_formd holds 2008q1-2026q3).

Verdict: answerable. Every figure is a Form D Item 13 amount as filed; the top-20 share is computed per calendar year (stated above) and the 20 largest are listed overall. Dropped rows: 0 (every filing has an EDGAR accession).


## Lead 12: Funds and nonprofits reporting exposure to Anthropic

Search: `ILIKE '%anthropic%'` on every name column (adv_funds.fund_name, sec_formd.issuer_name, sec_formd_offerings, sec_formd_people.name, sec_formd_parties.name, adv_filings legal/business name, adv_owners.name, iapd_firms, bd_firms, sec_company_links, organizations.name/dba, related_orgs.related_name (Schedule R), contractors (Part VII Section B / Schedule O), biz_entities.name, grant recipient tables) and full-text `to_tsquery('anthropic')` on the indexed 990 text (research.filing_text by field, research.org_text). 'anthropic pbc' / 'anthropic, pbc' are covered by the substring match. No Form 990 Schedule D Part VII/VIII investment table is loaded (only schedule_j exists), so 990 investment exposure can only surface through narrative text.

### SQL
```sql
SELECT f.fund_id, f.fund_name, f.crd, f.year, f.fund_type, f.gav, f.state, f.country, a.legal_name, f.filing_id FROM adv_funds f JOIN adv_filings a ON a.filing_id=f.filing_id WHERE f.fund_name ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY f.year DESC, f.gav DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT accession, cik, issuer_name, fund_type, industry, filing_date, is_amendment, offering_amount, amount_sold, investors, state, offering_indefinite FROM sec_formd WHERE issuer_name ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY filing_date

SELECT root_accession, cik, issuer_name, fund_type, first_date, latest_date, filings, latest_offering, latest_sold, investors, is_spv, family FROM sec_formd_offerings WHERE issuer_name ~* '\manthropic\M' OR family ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY first_date

SELECT p.accession, p.name, p.relationships, d.issuer_name, d.filing_date, d.cik FROM sec_formd_people p JOIN sec_formd d ON d.accession=p.accession WHERE p.name ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT id, kind, key, name, state, filings, last_date FROM sec_formd_parties WHERE name ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT filing_id, crd, legal_name, business_name, year, kind, aum FROM adv_filings WHERE legal_name ~* '\manthropic\M' OR business_name ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY year DESC

SELECT o.filing_id, o.crd, o.year, o.name, o.entity_kind, o.title, o.ownership_code, a.legal_name FROM adv_owners o JOIN adv_filings a ON a.filing_id=o.filing_id WHERE o.name ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY o.year DESC

SELECT crd, legal_name, business_name, state, status, other_names FROM iapd_firms WHERE legal_name ~* '\manthropic\M' OR business_name ~* '\manthropic\M' OR other_names::text ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT cik, name, crd, state FROM bd_firms WHERE name ~* '\manthropic\M' OR names::text ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT cik, kind, key, name FROM sec_company_links WHERE name ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT ein, name, state, latest_filing_year, latest_total_revenue, latest_total_assets FROM organizations WHERE name ~* '\manthropic\M' OR dba ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT r.filing_id, r.ein, r.related_ein, r.related_name, f.tax_year, f.org_name, f.object_id FROM related_orgs r JOIN filings f ON f.id=r.filing_id WHERE r.related_name ~* '\manthropic\M'

SELECT c.filing_id, c.ein, c.tax_year, c.name_raw, c.compensation, c.service_description, f.org_name, f.object_id FROM contractors c JOIN filings f ON f.id=c.filing_id WHERE c.name_norm ~* '\manthropic\M' OR c.name_raw ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY c.compensation DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT state, entity_id, name, entity_type, status, formed, jurisdiction, source, ein FROM biz_entities WHERE name ~* '\manthropic\M' ORDER BY formed NULLS LAST LIMIT 200

SELECT recipient_name, filing_id, ein, tax_year, purpose, recipient_ein FROM public.grants WHERE recipient_name ~* '\manthropic\M' LIMIT 200

SELECT recipient_name_norm, filing_id, ein, tax_year, purpose, recipient_ein FROM public.grants WHERE recipient_name_norm ~* '\manthropic\M' LIMIT 200

SELECT recipient_name, recipient_ein FROM public.usa_recipient_totals WHERE recipient_name ~* '\manthropic\M' LIMIT 200

SELECT DISTINCT ON (t.filing_id, t.field) t.filing_id, t.ein, t.tax_year, t.form_type, t.field, f.org_name, f.object_id, f.total_assets_eoy, f.superseded,
  substring(t.text from '(?i).{0,220}anthropic.{0,220}') AS snippet
FROM research.filing_text t JOIN filings f ON f.id=t.filing_id
WHERE t.field <> 'all' AND t.tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','anthropic') ORDER BY t.filing_id, t.field LIMIT 400

SELECT count(*) FROM research.filing_text WHERE field='all' AND tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','anthropic')

SELECT ein, name, state, max_revenue, last_year, substring(text from '(?i).{0,200}anthropic.{0,200}') FROM research.org_text WHERE tsv @@ to_tsquery('english','anthropic') ORDER BY max_revenue DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 300
```

Verdict: partly answerable. Fund-name and SPV exposure (Form ADV Schedule D 7.B.(1), Form D) and 990 narrative/contractor/Schedule R mentions are answerable from the data above. Dollar exposure of nonprofits' endowments to Anthropic shares is NOT answerable: Form 990 Schedule D Part VII (investments - other securities) is not loaded, and Form ADV/Form D do not list portfolio holdings; it would need Schedule D Part VII line items (IRS e-file XML, IRS990ScheduleD/OtherSecuritiesGrp) or Form 13F (not applicable to private company stock). Dropped: 0 traceable rows dropped; superseded 990 text rows excluded as noted.


## Lead 13: Top 50 donors to 2026-cycle federal super PACs and their nonprofit ties

Universe: 427,013 itemized receipts (tran_type 10/11/15/15C/15E, memo entries excluded, refunds excluded) to the 2,668 committees with cmte_type 'O' in fec_committees cycle 2026, from 86,503 distinct donor name+zip combinations, dated 2024-10-16 to 2026-10-14; gross total $2,691,871,110. Donor = name_norm+zip5 as reported on FEC Schedule A, so super PACs and 501(c)(4)s that fund other super PACs appear as donors (Majority Forward, One Nation, Win for America...). Nonprofit roles come from fec_person_links (FURP's FEC-to-people links) or, when no link exists, an exact person_clusters.name_norm match (labelled 'name_norm match'); organizational donors are matched to organizations.name_norm ('org name match'). 'Major funder' = Schedule B contributor rows in the donors table with the same name_norm. Each row shows the latest filing per organization; role lists are capped at 6 in the table (full list in JSON).

```sql
-- top
WITH sp AS (SELECT cmte_id FROM fec_committees WHERE cycle=2026 AND cmte_type='O')
SELECT c.name_norm, c.zip5, max(c.name) AS name, sum(c.amount) AS total, count(*) AS n,
       max(c.entity_type) AS entity_type, max(c.employer), max(c.occupation), max(c.city), max(c.state)
FROM fec_contributions_2026 c JOIN sp ON sp.cmte_id=c.cmte_id
WHERE c.amount>0 AND coalesce(c.memo_cd,'')<>'X' AND c.tran_type IN ('10','11','15','15C','15E')
GROUP BY c.name_norm, c.zip5 ORDER BY total DESC LIMIT 50

-- total_count
WITH sp AS (SELECT cmte_id FROM fec_committees WHERE cycle=2026 AND cmte_type='O')
SELECT count(*), count(DISTINCT (c.name_norm, c.zip5)), min(tran_date), max(tran_date), sum(amount)
FROM fec_contributions_2026 c JOIN sp ON sp.cmte_id=c.cmte_id
WHERE c.amount>0 AND coalesce(c.memo_cd,'')<>'X' AND c.tran_type IN ('10','11','15','15C','15E')

-- committees
SELECT c.cmte_id, k.name, sum(c.amount) FROM fec_contributions_2026 c
JOIN fec_committees k ON k.cycle=2026 AND k.cmte_id=c.cmte_id AND k.cmte_type='O'
WHERE c.name_norm=%s AND c.zip5 IS NOT DISTINCT FROM %s AND c.amount>0 AND coalesce(c.memo_cd,'')<>'X' AND c.tran_type IN ('10','11','15','15C','15E')
GROUP BY 1,2 ORDER BY 3 DESC LIMIT 3

-- biggest_txn
SELECT c.sub_id, c.image_num, c.amount, c.tran_date, c.cmte_id FROM fec_contributions_2026 c
JOIN fec_committees k ON k.cycle=2026 AND k.cmte_id=c.cmte_id AND k.cmte_type='O'
WHERE c.name_norm=%s AND c.zip5 IS NOT DISTINCT FROM %s AND c.amount>0 AND coalesce(c.memo_cd,'')<>'X' AND c.tran_type IN ('10','11','15','15C','15E')
ORDER BY c.amount DESC, c.tran_date LIMIT 1

-- links
SELECT DISTINCT l.cluster_id FROM fec_person_links l
JOIN fec_contributions_2026 c ON c.cycle=l.cycle AND c.sub_id=l.sub_id
WHERE l.cycle=2026 AND c.name_norm=%s AND c.zip5 IS NOT DISTINCT FROM %s

-- cluster_by_name
SELECT id FROM person_clusters WHERE name_norm=%s LIMIT 6

-- roles
SELECT DISTINCT ON (p.ein) p.ein, coalesce(o.name, f.org_name), p.title, p.is_officer, p.is_director, p.is_trustee, p.tax_year, p.filing_id, f.object_id, f.form_type, m.cluster_id
FROM person_cluster_members m JOIN people p ON p.id=m.person_id JOIN filings f ON f.id=p.filing_id LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=p.ein
WHERE m.cluster_id = ANY(%s) ORDER BY p.ein, p.tax_year DESC

-- funder
SELECT d.name_norm, d.ein, coalesce(o.name, f.org_name), d.tax_year, d.total_amount, d.filing_id, f.object_id, f.form_type
FROM donors d JOIN filings f ON f.id=d.filing_id LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=d.ein
WHERE d.name_norm = ANY(%s) ORDER BY d.total_amount DESC

-- org_match
SELECT name_norm, ein, name FROM organizations WHERE name_norm = ANY(%s)
```

**Total rows behind the list:** 427,013 receipts / 86,503 distinct donors; cycle 2026 (transactions dated 2024-10-16 to 2026-10-14). Dropped for lack of a traceable source record: 0. Note: JEFF YASS appears twice because the FEC file spells him two ways ('YASS, JEFF MR.' and 'YASS, JEFF'); combined he gave $79,000,000.

**Verdict:** partly answerable — the donor ranking and committee breakdown are answerable (FEC Schedule A); the nonprofit ties are partial: fec_person_links covers only 56,872 of the 2026 receipts, name-only matches can be wrong for common names (Reid Hoffman matched more than 5 clusters and was left unlinked; Paul Singer matched none), and organizational donors are linked to nonprofits by exact name only.


## Lead 14: Defense-contractor executives and PACs giving to current Republican senators (cycles 2024 and 2026)

Contractor list used: the known top-10 DoD list (Lockheed Martin, RTX/Raytheon incl. Collins Aerospace and Pratt & Whitney, General Dynamics incl. Gulfstream, Boeing, Northrop Grumman, L3Harris, HII/Huntington Ingalls incl. Newport News and Ingalls Shipbuilding, Leidos, BAE Systems, General Electric/GE Aerospace); employer patterns are in the JSON. Senators: the 53 Republican members of the 119th Congress Senate as of November 2026 (hard-coded last name + state list, in the JSON as senator_list), resolved to FEC candidate IDs in fec_candidates (office='S', party='REP', office_state and last-name match, any cycle) — 56 candidate records resolved (a few senators have two FEC IDs), none unresolved. The FEC 'ici' incumbent flag was not used because in cycle 2026 it still marks ex-senators such as Flake, Corker, Portman, Romney, Rubio and Vance. Committees: 69 committees in fec_committees cycles 2024/2026 with those cand_ids and designation P (principal), A (authorized) or D (leadership PAC). Executives: entity_type IND, employer ILIKE a contractor pattern AND occupation ILIKE any of ['%chief%', '%president%', '%officer%', '%vp%', '%vice%', '%director%', '%executive%']. PACs: receipts from non-individual contributors whose name matches a contractor pattern or whose other_id is one of 14 committees with a matching connected_org/name (list in JSON). Memo entries and refunds excluded; dates 2023-01-01 to 2026-12-31.

**Data gap, stated up front:** the PAC query returns 0 rows. FURP's fec_contributions holds the FEC 'indiv' bulk file (itemized receipts from individuals, filer = recipient committee) plus a thin set of non-individual rows; the contractor PACs' own records (e.g. Lockheed Martin PAC C00303024) contain only their receipts from employees, and there are no tran_type 24K PAC-to-candidate rows. PAC giving to senators therefore needs the FEC 'pas2' bulk file (contributions from committees to candidates), which is not loaded. The per-senator table below is executives only.

```sql
-- senators
SELECT DISTINCT ON (cand_id) cand_id, name, office_state, pcc FROM fec_candidates
WHERE office='S' AND party='REP' AND office_state=%s AND name ILIKE %s AND cand_id LIKE 'S%%' ORDER BY cand_id, cycle DESC

-- committees
SELECT DISTINCT k.cmte_id, k.name, k.designation, k.cand_id FROM fec_committees k
WHERE k.cycle IN (2024,2026) AND k.cand_id = ANY(%s) AND k.designation IN ('P','A','D')

-- exec
SELECT c.cycle, c.sub_id, c.image_num, c.cmte_id, c.name, c.employer, c.occupation, c.amount, c.tran_date, c.city, c.state, c.tran_type
FROM fec_contributions c
WHERE c.cycle IN (2024,2026) AND c.cmte_id = ANY(%s) AND c.entity_type='IND' AND c.amount>0 AND coalesce(c.memo_cd,'')<>'X'
  AND c.tran_type IN ('10','11','15','15C','15E') AND c.tran_date BETWEEN '2023-01-01' AND '2026-12-31'
  AND (c.employer ILIKE ANY(%s)) AND (c.occupation ILIKE ANY(%s))

-- contractor_pacs
SELECT DISTINCT cmte_id, name, connected_org FROM fec_committees
WHERE cycle IN (2024,2026) AND cmte_type NOT IN ('H','S','P') AND (connected_org ILIKE ANY(%s) OR name ILIKE ANY(%s))

-- pac
SELECT c.cycle, c.sub_id, c.image_num, c.cmte_id, c.name, c.other_id, c.amount, c.tran_date, c.tran_type, c.entity_type
FROM fec_contributions c
WHERE c.cycle IN (2024,2026) AND c.cmte_id = ANY(%s) AND coalesce(c.entity_type,'')<>'IND' AND c.amount>0 AND coalesce(c.memo_cd,'')<>'X'
  AND c.tran_type IN ('10','11','15','15C','15E','24K') AND c.tran_date BETWEEN '2023-01-01' AND '2026-12-31'
  AND (c.name ILIKE ANY(%s) OR c.other_id = ANY(%s))
```
