### Top PAC transactions

(none in the database — see data gap above)

**Total rows behind the list:** 257 executive receipts totalling $239,145 (dated 2023-02-03 to 2026-06-24) and 0 PAC receipts; 16 of 53 senators received something. Cycles 2024 and 2026. Dropped: 0 (every executive row carries an FEC sub_id and image number).

**Verdict:** partly answerable — the executive half is answerable (FEC Schedule A itemized individual receipts; caveat: employer/occupation are self-reported free text, so titles like 'Executive' or 'Director' are taken at face value and board-level titles that do not match the patterns are missed); the PAC half is not answerable with current data (needs the FEC pas2 'contributions from committees to candidates' bulk file for cycles 2024 and 2026).


## Lead 15: US foundation grants to European NGOs and to US 'Friends of' charities of European institutions

Three tables. (A) Form 990 Schedule F Part II rows in intl_grants_abroad with region starting 'Europe' (grants to organizations outside the US, by region; filer-level totals; Schedule F is attached to Form 990, not 990-PF, so these filers are mostly public charities and 'Friends of' intermediaries rather than private foundations; FURP's Schedule F coverage is tax years 2023-2024 only). (B) Schedule F Part I rows for Europe whose description mentions grants. (C) Form 990-PF Part XV grants (grants.source='990PF-XV', filings.form_type 990PF, summary rows excluded) whose recipient name starts 'american friends of' or 'friends of' and contains a European place/institution keyword; Israel/Hebrew/Jerusalem and US namesakes (Paris TX, Athens GA, Dublin OH...) are excluded by a second regex; both regexes are below and in the JSON. The matched keyword is shown so false positives can be spotted. Direct 990-PF grants to NGOs located in Europe are not isolated: 990-PF Part XV has no country field.

```sql
-- sched_f_part2
SELECT i.ein, coalesce(o.name, max(f.org_name)) AS name, sum(i.amount) AS total, count(*) AS n, min(i.tax_year), max(i.tax_year),
       (array_agg(i.object_id ORDER BY i.amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1] AS object_id, max(f.form_type), (array_agg(i.description ORDER BY i.amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1], (array_agg(i.region ORDER BY i.amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1]
FROM intl_grants_abroad i LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=i.ein LEFT JOIN filings f ON f.object_id::text=i.object_id
WHERE i.region ILIKE 'Europe%' AND i.part LIKE 'Part II%' GROUP BY i.ein, o.name ORDER BY total DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 20

-- sched_f_part2_count
SELECT count(*), count(DISTINCT ein), min(tax_year), max(tax_year), sum(amount) FROM intl_grants_abroad WHERE region ILIKE 'Europe%' AND part LIKE 'Part II%'

-- sched_f_part1_grants
SELECT i.ein, coalesce(o.name, max(f.org_name)) AS name, sum(i.amount) AS total, count(*) AS n, min(i.tax_year), max(i.tax_year),
       (array_agg(i.object_id ORDER BY i.amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1] AS object_id, max(f.form_type), (array_agg(i.description ORDER BY i.amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1]
FROM intl_grants_abroad i LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=i.ein LEFT JOIN filings f ON f.object_id::text=i.object_id
WHERE i.region ILIKE 'Europe%' AND i.part LIKE 'Part I%' AND i.part NOT LIKE 'Part II%' AND i.description ILIKE '%grant%' GROUP BY i.ein, o.name ORDER BY total DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 20

-- sched_f_part1_count
SELECT count(*), count(DISTINCT ein), min(tax_year), max(tax_year), sum(amount) FROM intl_grants_abroad WHERE region ILIKE 'Europe%' AND part LIKE 'Part I%' AND part NOT LIKE 'Part II%' AND description ILIKE '%grant%'

-- friends_pairs
SELECT g.ein, coalesce(o.name, max(f.org_name)) AS grantor, g.recipient_name_norm, max(g.recipient_name) AS recipient, sum(g.cash_amount) AS total, count(*) AS n,
       min(g.tax_year), max(g.tax_year), (array_agg(g.filing_id ORDER BY g.cash_amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1], (array_agg(f.object_id ORDER BY g.cash_amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1],
       max(g.cash_amount), max(g.city), max(g.state), (array_agg(g.purpose ORDER BY g.cash_amount DESC NULLS LAST))[1], substring(g.recipient_name_norm from %s) AS kw
FROM grants g JOIN filings f ON f.id=g.filing_id LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.ein=g.ein
WHERE g.source='990PF-XV' AND f.form_type ILIKE '990PF%%' AND coalesce(g.is_summary,false)=false
  AND (g.recipient_name_norm LIKE 'american friends of%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm LIKE 'friends of%%')
  AND g.recipient_name_norm ~* %s AND NOT g.recipient_name_norm ~* %s
GROUP BY g.ein, o.name, g.recipient_name_norm ORDER BY total DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 25

-- friends_count
SELECT count(*), count(DISTINCT g.ein), count(DISTINCT g.recipient_name_norm), min(g.tax_year), max(g.tax_year), sum(g.cash_amount)
FROM grants g JOIN filings f ON f.id=g.filing_id
WHERE g.source='990PF-XV' AND f.form_type ILIKE '990PF%%' AND coalesce(g.is_summary,false)=false
  AND (g.recipient_name_norm LIKE 'american friends of%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm LIKE 'friends of%%')
  AND g.recipient_name_norm ~* %s AND NOT g.recipient_name_norm ~* %s

-- friends_recipients
SELECT g.recipient_name_norm, max(g.recipient_name), sum(g.cash_amount), count(*), count(DISTINCT g.ein), min(g.tax_year), max(g.tax_year), substring(g.recipient_name_norm from %s)
FROM grants g JOIN filings f ON f.id=g.filing_id
WHERE g.source='990PF-XV' AND f.form_type ILIKE '990PF%%' AND coalesce(g.is_summary,false)=false
  AND (g.recipient_name_norm LIKE 'american friends of%%' OR g.recipient_name_norm LIKE 'friends of%%')
  AND g.recipient_name_norm ~* %s AND NOT g.recipient_name_norm ~* %s
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 3 DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 25
```

EU regex: `\m(oxford|cambridge|louvre|british|london|paris|versailles|edinburgh|dublin|eton|imperial college|kings college|king s college|st andrews|covent garden|royal (academy|opera|ballet|shakespeare|society|college|botanic|albert|marsden|horticultural|air force|navy|parks|institution|national)|glyndebourne|vienna|berlin|munich|rome|florence|venice|milan|madrid|barcelona|prado|amsterdam|rijksmuseum|geneva|zurich|swiss|switzerland|france|french|italy|italian|germany|german|england|english|scotland|scottish|ireland|irish|wales|welsh|netherlands|dutch|belgium|belgian|sweden|swedish|norway|norwegian|denmark|danish|finland|finnish|poland|polish|czech|hungary|hungarian|greece|greek|portugal|portuguese|spain|spanish|austria|austrian|ukraine|ukrainian|chatsworth|hermitage|united kingdom|europe|european|bulgaria|romania|serbia|croatia|lithuania|latvia|estonia|slovakia|slovenia|iceland|luxembourg|monaco|vatican|sorbonne|salzburg|bayreuth|durham|westminster|canterbury|warsaw|krakow|prague|budapest|athens|lisbon|copenhagen|stockholm|oslo|helsinki|brussels|bruges|strasbourg|lyon|bordeaux|normandy|brittany|tuscany|sicily|naples|turin|bologna|verona|sardinia|andalusia|catalonia|flemish|hamburg|frankfurt|cologne|dresden|leipzig|heidelberg|bavaria|tyrol|cornwall|yorkshire|glasgow|aberdeen|belfast|cork|galway|liverpool|manchester|bristol|dulwich|highgate|kew|blenheim|wimbledon|stratford upon avon|ashmolean|fitzwilliam|bodleian|v a|victoria and albert|uffizi|la scala|teatro|opera de paris|comedie|mozarteum|bergen|tromso|chernobyl|moldova|kosovo|bosnia|albania|macedonia|montenegro|malta|cyprus|lourdes|taize|assisi|siena|lucca|cinque terre|padua|ravenna|pisa|genoa|monte cassino|trier|wittenberg)\M`

Exclusion regex: `\m(israel|israeli|hebrew|jerusalem|tel aviv|haifa|weizmann|technion|bar ilan|ben gurion|magen david|yad vashem|new england|new london|london ohio|london ky|london kentucky|english language|english speaking|english as a|rome ga|rome georgia|rome ny|rome new york|paris tx|paris texas|paris il|paris tn|paris ky|athens ga|athens georgia|athens ohio|athens tn|athens al|manchester nh|manchester vt|manchester ct|manchester tn|florence al|florence sc|florence alabama|florence south carolina|florence ky|naples fl|naples florida|venice fl|venice beach|venice california|milan tn|milan mi|milan ohio|dublin ohio|dublin ca|dublin california|dublin ga|dublin georgia|dublin va|durham nc|durham north carolina|cambridge ma|cambridge massachusetts|cambridge md|oxford ms|oxford mississippi|oxford ohio|oxford ct|oxford nc|oxford al|oxford pa|oxford mi|berlin nh|berlin md|berlin ct|berlin wi|berlin vt|vienna va|vienna virginia|vienna wv|geneva ny|geneva il|geneva ohio|geneva wi|bristol ct|bristol tn|bristol va|bristol ri|bristol pa|liverpool ny|glasgow ky|glasgow mt|aberdeen md|aberdeen sd|aberdeen wa|aberdeen nc|cork n|belfast me|belfast maine|westminster md|westminster co|westminster ca|westminster college|westminster school|westminster abbey|canterbury school|warsaw in|warsaw ny|warsaw va|cologne mn|hamburg ny|hamburg pa|frankfurt ky|frankfort|lisbon oh|lisbon ct|lisbon nd|lisbon me|copenhagen ny|stockholm wi|oslo mn|brussels wi|hermitage pa|hermitage tn|normandy park|bavaria il|greek orthodox|greek american|irish american|italian american|polish american|german american|hungarian american|ukrainian catholic|ukrainian orthodox|french american|swiss american|norwegian american|danish american|swedish american|british american|scottish american|dutch reformed|first (german|english|dutch|french|swedish|norwegian|danish|polish|irish|italian|greek|hungarian)|german (lutheran|evangelical)|new york|portland|portugal cove)\M`

**Total rows behind table C:** 3,292 grants from 966 foundations to 582 distinct recipient names, tax years 2009–2025, sum $100,254,499. Dropped for missing object_id: 0 (C), 0 (A), 0 (B).

**Verdict:** partly answerable. 'Friends of' grants (C) are answerable from 990-PF Part XV with a keyword filter that needs human review. Schedule F (A, B) gives region-level Europe totals but no recipient names (the IRS schema lists only the region) and covers Form 990 filers for 2023-2024 only, not 990-PF foundations. Direct 990-PF grants to European NGOs are not answerable as a clean list: 990-PF Part XV has no country field, so it would need country parsing of recipient city/state text or an external register of European NGOs.
